JetBlue Help Guide

JetBlue’s own help documentation, gathered into one document

2026-08-02

JetBlue Help Guide

This guide gathers JetBlue’s own help documentation, as it stood on 2 August 2026.

Of the five major airlines, JetBlue is the one whose help could be gathered at all. American refused every request, including its special-assistance page. United would not answer. Delta and Alaska publish real writing but link to almost none of it, keeping their help behind a search box that a reader cannot browse and a gatherer cannot follow.

What was left out. The harvest also collected some five hundred route pages – flights from one city to another – which are advertising rather than help. They are not here.

What is here. The help centre, the accessibility material, airport and onboard information, and the legal documents a passenger actually needs, including the contract of carriage and the tarmac delay plan. The accessibility pages are the reason this volume exists: mobility devices, service animals, seating accommodations and boarding assistance are exactly what nobody wants to be searching for at a gate.

One caution. Airline policies change often, and fees and rules more often still. The date above is the date this was taken; check jetblue.com before relying on anything that turns on money or on a deadline.

This document holds 4 categories, 68 articles and about 71,265 words.

Flying with JetBlue

EvenMore®

Enjoy  extra legroom, a seat toward the front, and a bundle of premium perks on board and at the airport. Available on all JetBlue flights—with a choice of multiple fare options. Select EvenMore when shopping for flights, or add it to an existing Main fare in Manage Trips or at check-in. 

More space. More speed. More service.

Get a leg up (& out).

EvenMore gives you extra legroom and a prime location toward the front, for easier boarding & deplaning.   

Keep calm & carry on.

Get your stuff stowed and get settled sooner with Group 2 boarding and dedicated overhead bin space1. 

Even more food & drink.

Free inflight beer, wine and liquor (21+) and a premium snack offering on eligible flights2 (that’s in addition to JetBlue’s free brand-name snacks + drinks). 

An elevated airport experience.

Fly to your gate faster with an expedited lane to the security checkpoint at dozens of airports. 

That’s in addition to the award-winning service and perks that come included with every JetBlue experience

Free high-speed wi-fi³ for everyone, on every plane.

Get connected

Free live TV³, movies & more at every seat.

Free brand-name snacks + drinks.

FAQs

Got questions? We’ve got answers.

EvenMore

  • Do Mosaic members get EvenMore at no extra charge?

    EvenMore is included as a Mosaic Signature Perk.

    • Mosaic 1 members can select EvenMore® for free beginning at check-in (pending availability), excluding Main Base fares. Just select any available EvenMore® seat from the seat map when you check in online, on the app or at the airport kiosk starting 24 hours before departure.  
    • Mosaic 2 and above can select EvenMore® for free during booking (pending availability), by choosing a Main fare (excluding Main Base) and adding EvenMore® during seat selection.

  • What are the benefits of EvenMore?

    EvenMore offers premium perks, including:

    • Extra legroom
    • Early boarding
    • A prime seat closer to the front of the plane (for faster deplaning, too)
    • Dedicated bin space
    • Free alcoholic drinks² & a premium snack offering²
    • Priority security at 30+ airports

  • Do all flights have EvenMore?

    All JetBlue-operated flights now offer the option of EvenMore. Please note that flights under 250 miles (or with no inflight service) do not include the free alcoholic drinks and premium snack offering, but do include the rest of the perks—extra legroom, early boarding, location in first few rows, dedicated overhead bin space and (where available) priority security.

  • Which seats include EvenMore?

    Seats in the first few rows of the plane (or the first few behind Mint, in a plane that has Mint) include EvenMore.

    For a more detailed view, see the seat maps for the specific flight.

  • Can EvenMore be combined with a Main Base fare?

    Yes. When booking a Main Base fare, you can purchase EvenMore by selecting it on the seat map.

  • How can I book EvenMore?

    All JetBlue flights offer EvenMore®. If it’s available on your flight, you’ll see it in the search results during booking and can select it then. You can also book EvenMore from the seat map when managing your trip or checking in.

  • Can I book an EvenMore Base fare?

    EvenMore Base fares will become available in the coming weeks.

  • How much does EvenMore cost?

    The cost of EvenMore varies by route. To check the price on a particular flight, simply select EvenMore from the seat map during booking or check-in. 

  • Can I book EvenMore with TrueBlue points or JetBlue travel credits?

    Yes, you can use TrueBlue points or JetBlue travel credits to pay for EvenMore® when it’s selected at time of original booking. TrueBlue points and travel credits can’t be applied to EvenMore® if it’s added later in seat selection.

  • Do Mosaic members get EvenMore at no extra charge?

    Yes. Mosaic members can select EvenMore for free (for themselves and eligible travel companions on the same itinerary), just as they did with Even More Space.  

  • Can I book EvenMore as part of a JetBlue Vacations package?

    Yes. EvenMore seats can be purchased as an add-on to a JetBlue Vacations package. Please allow 2 hours after you’ve completed your booking, and select EvenMore from the seat map while managing your trip.  

  • Are EvenMore fees refundable?

    Currently, all EvenMore seat fees are refunded to the original form of payment if you need to change or cancel your flight. They’re also refunded if JetBlue cancels your flight due to an operational disruption and can’t rebook you in an EvenMore seat on your re-accommodated flight. However, all seat fees are forfeited if you paid for EvenMore and later select a non-EvenMore seat, or if you pre-reserved EvenMore and do not show up for your flight without cancelling. 

  • How can I book a seat in an exit row?

    Exit-row seats are now listed on the seat map as extra legroom seats. You can select them for an additional fee during booking, when managing your trip or at check-in (pending availability). The fee is waived for Mosaic members. Exit row seats include extra legroom but do not include the premium perks of EvenMore or the former Even More Space. 

    Due to safety regulations, there are age, mobility and other requirements for customers sitting in exit rows, including the ability to assist in an emergency, communicate in English, and more.

    See all exit row requirements and restrictions.

  • What are the inflight drinks and snacks included with EvenMore? 

    In addition to the free snacks & drinks that are part of the JetBlue Main experience, EvenMore customers can also order up to 3 free alcoholic drinks (must be 21+) and they also get a free bag of Tiny Tate’s Chocolate Chip Cookies, which are just like Tate’s signature chocolate chip cookies, but bite-sized for snacking on the go.  

    Please note that this inflight alcoholic drink perk and premium snack offering are not available on flights under 250 miles or on flights with no inflight service. 

Next: Mint

  1. You can bring 1 free carry-on bag to be placed in the overhead bin (space permitting) + 1 personal item to be placed all the way under the seat in front of you.

  2. Does not apply to flights under 250 miles or with no inflight service. Max 3 free alcoholic drinks per flight, 21+. Can’t be combined with other free drink perks or offers. (Transatlantic flights already include free beer, wine & liquor.)

  3. Availability and coverage area may vary by aircraft. 

  4. Carry-on bags may not exceed 22” L (55.88 cm) x 14” W (35.56 cm) x 9” H (22.86 cm).

  5. Personal items (like a purse, daypack or laptop bag) must fit under the seat in front of you and may not exceed 17” L (43.2 cm) x 13” W (33 cm) x 8” H (20.32 cm). Travelers who have added a pet to their fare for travel may bring a carry-on bag. Your approved pet carrier counts as your personal item.

  6. First checked bag included on all transatlantic routes as well as flights to/from CTG, GYE, MDE, PAP, GEO, BAQ, & CLO.

  7. Does not apply to same-day switches. Subject to fare difference and fare rules applicable on date of change. Funds will be in the form of a JetBlue travel credit, valid for 12 months from original ticketing date. See details on expiration date here.

  8. A change/cancellation fee will apply to the change/cancellation of any Base fare award redemption booking. Payment by credit card at the time of change/cancellation is required; points will be re-deposited to the member’s account following payment.

  9. Same-day switches cannot be completed by agency. Same-day switches can only be completed directly by JetBlue. Same-day switches can be made after midnight on day of travel. No fare difference applies.

  10. EvenMore Base does not include seat selection at booking. Seat selection becomes available at check-in. If you do not select a seat a check-in, one will be automatically assigned.

Experience the Extras

Preferred & extra legroom seats

Want to stretch out with the extra legroom of an exit-row or over-wing seat? Or, enjoy a seat toward the front without all the perks of EvenMore? You can book a Main fare and select either an extra legroom seat or a preferred seat for a fee (free for Mosaic members). Just look for them on the seat map during seat selection.  

Priority security

Fly to your gate faster with an expedited lane to the security checkpoint at 30+ airports.  

Priority security is automatically included at no extra charge with our EvenMore® and Mint fares, as well as for Mosaic members. Priority security can be purchased for each traveler when booking or checking in by adding it in the “extras” section.  

You can also pay for priority security with TrueBlue points any time before check-in in Manage Trips on jetblue.com, or in My Trips on the JetBlue app.

Other Extras Can be purchased a la carte for each traveler during booking or check-in—or anytime in between in Manage Trips.  

Booking Extra Seats

Whether you want more physical distance from other travelers or you’re toting a large musical instrument, booking an extra seat next to you (or even blocking out entire rows) can be done with a couple extra clicks.

FAQs

Got questions? We’ve got answers.

Preferred & Extra Legroom Seats

  • What’s the difference between preferred seats, extra legroom seats and EvenMore?

     

    • Preferred seats are preferred by those who may want to deplane faster or be closer to the front, without the extra legroom or premium perks of EvenMore. On flights with EvenMore, preferred seats will be in the rows just behind it. 
    • Extra legroom seats are for those who love some extra stretch, but without the premium perks of EvenMore. On most flights they’re located mid-plane &/or in exit rows. 
    • EvenMore seats are located in the first few rows (or the first few behind Mint, on a plane that has Mint). But, more than a seat or a location, EvenMore gives you premium perks like early boarding, extra legroom, dedicated bin space, free alcoholic drinks² and a premium snack offering², plus priority security at 30+ airports. EvenMore is available on all JetBlue planes.

    No matter where you sit, you’ll enjoy our award-winning service and inflight experience, including free high-speed wi-fi, seatback entertainment and free brand-name snacks + drinks.  

    For specific seat locations for your flight, please see the seat map in Manage trips.

  • Where are preferred and extra legroom seats located?

    Preferred seats are usually located toward the front. On flights with EvenMore, preferred seats will be in the rows just behind it. 

    Extra legroom seats are usually located mid-plane.

    For the most accurate info, please see the seat maps for your specific flight in Manage trips. 

  • Do all planes have preferred and extra legroom seats?

    Yes. All JetBlue planes have preferred seats and most (except for some A321s) have extra legroom seats. All JetBlue planes also offer EvenMore.

  • How do I book preferred and extra legroom seats?

    You can select preferred or extra legroom (pending availability) from the seat map when booking, managing your trip or checking in.  

  • Can I combine a preferred or extra legroom seat with a Main Basic fare?

    Yes. When booking a Main Basic fare, you can purchase a preferred or extra legroom seat by selecting it on the seat map.

  • Do Mosaic members get preferred or extra legroom seats at no extra charge?

    Yes. Mosaic members can select preferred or extra legroom seats for free (for themselves and eligible travel companions on the same itinerary), just as they did with preferred seats previously.

  • Are fees for preferred or extra legroom seats refundable?

    Currently, all seat fees are refunded to the original form of payment should you need to change or cancel your flight. Charges are also refunded if JetBlue cancels your flight due to an operational disruption and cannot rebook you in the seat you purchased on your re-accommodated flight. However, all charges are forfeited if you paid for a preferred or extra legroom seat and later select a different seat, or if you do not show up for your flight without cancelling. 

Priority Security

  • What are the benefits of priority security?

    Priority security offers an expedited lane to the standard security checkpoint at 30+ U.S. and international airports. In addition, if you have priority security as a Mosaic member, or if you’re traveling on a Mint or EvenMore fare, all travelers on your itinerary can access priority security (where available).

  • How can I add priority security to my trip?

    There are a few ways to get priority security. 

    • Priority security is automatically included with your flight when you purchase a Mint or EvenMore fare. 
    • You can purchase priority security as a standalone from the Extras menu when you book a flight, manage your trip or check in. If you haven’t added priority security to your flight before you arrive at the airport—and priority security is offered at that airport—you can add it at the JetBlue check-in kiosk. 
    • You can enjoy priority security not just on a specific flight but all JetBlue-operated flights when you achieve Mosaic status, or by selecting priority security as one of the Perks You Pick as a TrueBlue member. 
    • You can also pay for priority security with TrueBlue points any time before check-in in Manage Trips on jetblue.com, or in My Trips on the JetBlue app.  

  • Can I purchase priority security at the airport?

    Yes, even if you haven’t added priority security to your flight before you arrive at the airport, if priority security is offered at that airport you can still add it at the JetBlue check-in kiosk.

  • Can I bring my travel companion(s) through the priority security lane?

    The answer is yes if you purchased a Mint or EvenMore fare, or you have priority security as a Mosaic member or a TrueBlue member who selected it as a perk. Eligible travelers booked under the same reservation and confirmation code can accompany you through priority security.  

    If you booked EvenMore or purchased priority security as a standalone, only you will be able to access the priority security lane.  

  • Which airports currently offer priority security?

    Priority security is available in 30+ airports on both sides of the Atlantic. To see if it’s available at your departure airport, please visit our Airport Info pages.

  • How do I take advantage of priority security at the airport?

    Access varies by airport location. At some airports, priority security signage will be visible at the security checkpoint entrance, while other airports may allow access to the premium or business/first class lanes. A JetBlue crewmember at each airport will be happy to assist with questions on how to take advantage of priority security. As long as your boarding pass indicates that you have priority security, you should be good to go.

  • What credentials are necessary to take advantage of priority security?

    Priority security will be indicated on your boarding pass, simply show your mobile or printed boarding pass to the attendant at the appropriate security checkpoint entrance. 

  • Do I need priority security if I already have TSA PreCheck?

    It depends on the airport and your situation.  

    TSA PreCheck offers expedited lanes and screening, but it’s not available at all airports. So, having priority security may give you an advantage at some airports, especially those outside the U.S. 

    In addition, if you’re a Mosaic member, or traveling on a Mint or EvenMore fare, all travelers on your itinerary can access priority security (where available), whereas each traveler must be enrolled in TSA PreCheck to access the TSA PreCheck lanes. 

Need help?

Main

Our original inflight experience—complete with seatback TVs, tech touches & tasty noshes—is available on all JetBlue flights, with a choice of multiple fare options. 

Make time fly.

Get on board & get online.

JetBlue is the only airline with fast, free wi-fi for everyone, on every plane. Just log in to our free Fly-Fi network to multi-task, message, browse, buy, like, listen, surf, stream and more. 

 

Screen time, your way.

Just plug in your personal wired earphones and enjoy hours of free live TV, movies and more on your seatback screen—even on transatlantic flights. 

Chow down.

Main includes free brand-name snacks + drinks, including freshly brewed Dunkin’ coffee. Transatlantic flights also include a free main meal, pre-arrival light bite & alcoholic drinks (21+)*. *Alcoholic drinks (21+), and our EatUp® menus of snack boxes and fresh foods are available for purchase on select flights. 

Juice up.

Keep your devices charged with power ports at every seat (or every row, depending on aircraft).

In addition to all the usual perks of Main, pondhoppers also get to enjoy a main meal from NYC’s Dig Inn, a pre-arrival light bite, and free beer, wine and liquor  (21+).

Mint®

Fly like nobody’s business in Mint and see why JetBlue was ranked #1 in Customer Satisfaction among First/Business Class Passengers for the second year in a row.¹ Available on all JetBlue transatlantic flights, and select coast-to-coast, Caribbean & Latin America flights.

Lie-flat seats & suites

Kick back. (All the way back.)

All Mint seats and suites convert to a fully lie-flat bed in seconds, complete with Tuft & Needle’s T&N Adaptive® Foam cushions, and a T&N-designed pillow & comforter. 

Suite dreams.

In search of neighbor-free nirvana? Every Mint seat on our A321LR or neo with Mint is a private suite with direct aisle access. Plus, rows 2 & 4 on our A321 Classic with Mint are suites. 

We don’t sleep on the details.

Feel right at home with a large touchscreen TV, plenty of in-seat power, mood lighting, and dedicated nooks for shoes, a laptop & more. And our amenities are a keeper—featuring essentials from Dr. Dennis Gross skincare in a collectible Caraa bag. 

For the travel overachiever.

The spacious front-row Mint Studio boasts the largest TV (22”!) on a U.S. airline, a mirrored vanity, even more storage, and a guest seat & table—to work, lounge or entertain a fellow Mint traveler. Available on our A321LR and A321neo. 

New flavors from New York City.

Savor a restaurant-inspired small plates menu–curated in partnership with FiDi’s Crown Shy (lunch/dinner) or Williamsburg’s Birdee (breakfast) on most flights*.

View Mint & special meal menus

Sip back & relax.

We’ve partnered with iconic speakeasy PDT (Please Don’t Tell) to raise the bar on open bar, with cocktails shaken on board. Plus, enjoy an artisan wine list curated by Grant Reynolds of Parcelle.

View current drink menu

Savor & Sleep™

Dine and drift off sooner with an express evening meal service on all coast-to-coast flights within the U.S. departing after 8pm. Also available as an option on all transatlantic lunch/dinner flights. 

View Savor & Sleep menu

Entertainment & Connectivity

Your living room. Only better.

Take content-mint to new heights with a 15” (or larger) TV, tons of on-demand movies and shows, live TV², meditation and sleepscapes from Open, and more.

A sound idea.

Transform your Mint seat into an inflight theater with noise-isolating headphones from Master & Dynamic (on transatlantic flights) or Thinksound (on all others). 

Free high-speed wi-fi

Make the most of your time on board with free high-speed wi-fi² and multiple in-seat power ports—plus wireless charging on our A321LR & A321neo.

Learn more about Fly-Fi®

More Travel Perks

Fly through the airport faster with dedicated Mint check-in and access to priority security³. 

Play, work & lounge at BlueHouse, with complimentary access up to 3 hours before your transatlantic Mint flight from JFK (and Boston, opening soon).

Be one of the first on the plane and get settled sooner, with Group 1 boarding.

Get 2 free checked bags and get on your way faster with priority bag handling, so they’re among the first to the carousel.

Mint FAQs

    • Can I book a Mint seat for my mother and a Main seat for myself on the same reservation?

      No, everyone on a reservation should be booked on the same experience. You will need to book two separate reservations if one of you flies Mint and the other books a Main seat.

    • If I’m booking a Mint seat, do I get priority security?

      Yes, priority security is included automatically for all Mint customers.

    • Do Mint customers pay for their second checked bag?

      No, like Mosaic Customers, Mint Customers get the second checked bag free.

    • Is there a government/military rate for Mint?

      No, government contracts do not allow for business class flights less than 14 hours in duration.

    • What are the features of the Mint experience?

      Mint seats offer the longest fully-flat bed (at up to 6’8”) in the U.S. domestic premium market.

    • What length are the Mint seats when they are in the “lie-flat” position?

      Our flat-bed seats are the longest in the domestic market at up to 6’8” long. Based on avg. length of lie-flat beds on domestic flights operated by U.S. airlines.

    • Can Mint seats be purchased with TrueBlue points?

      Yes, Mint seats are available for purchase using TrueBlue points.

    • Do I earn TrueBlue points at a higher rate when purchasing Mint flights?

      No, TrueBlue points are earned based on the fare paid, not on the experience.

    • If I purchase a Main flight with points and later decide to change to a Mint seat, can I use cash/credit to enhance my experience?

      No, the same form of payment would have to be used. Customers traveling on redemption tickets must use points to enjoy the Mint experience; customers traveling on revenue tickets must use cash/credit.

    • If I booked a Mint seat but choose to go standby on an earlier flight that doesn’t have Mint, will I get a credit?

      No, if you voluntarily go standby on an earlier flight you will forfeit the Mint experience with no compensation.

    • If I booked a Mint seat, can I do a same day change and confirm a seat on another flight that day?

      If there is another Mint flight on the same day of travel that has Mint seat availability, you may confirm seats after midnight local time for the same day change fee by calling 1-800-JETBLUE. See same-day change fee details.

    • If I booked a Main or EvenMore seat, can I do a same day change to Mint?

      No, same day change may be from Mint to Mint or from Mint to Main only. If Mint is available and you want to confirm Mint, you must exchange the ticket and pay any difference in fare.

    • Mint change/cancel fees

      There are no fees for changes or cancellations with a Mint fare. For changes, you can rebook to any flight through the end of our current schedule. Fare difference may apply.

      For voluntary cancellations, funds will be issued as a JetBlue Travel Bank credit. JetBlue Vacations bookings will be issued as JetBlue Vacations credit. Travel credits can be used for future travel to any JetBlue destination and for any traveler.

      To make changes, visit the Manage Trips or contact us for assistance.

    • Do carry-on bags go in the overhead for Mint?

      Yes, since there is no under-seat stowage, carry-on bags must be placed in the overhead bin for takeoff and landing.

    • Are carry-on requirements different for Mint customers?

      No, Mint customers are allowed the same number of carry-on items as Main customers. FAA/TSA regulations allow each customer to take one carry-on and one personal item onboard the aircraft.

    • What is the under seat size for storing carry-ons in Mint?

      There is no under-seat stowage in Mint; however, customers may still bring one carry-on and one personal item. Both items will need to meet the standard size restrictions and be stored in the overhead bin during takeoff and landing.

    • Are service animals allowed in Mint seating?

      Yes, JetBlue must permit Service Animals/Psychiatric Service Animals to accompany a customer with a disability at any seat in which the person sits, unless the animal obstructs an aisle or other areas that must remain unobstructed in order to facilitate an emergency evacuation. In JetBlue’s Mint seating, the customer will forfeit the use of the reclining feature in order to accommodate a service animal at the feet of the customer, outside a carrier. Carriers are not permitted and must be stowed in the overhead compartment during takeoff and landing. If the animal is small enough to sit comfortably on the customer’s lap, the reclining feature may be used. If the animal(s) are of a size that prevents an adjacent customer from utilizing the amenities of their seat, the customer traveling with the animal(s) may need to be re-accommodated in another seat.

    • Can a customer use a Portable Oxygen Concentrator in Mint seating?

      JetBlue will allow a customer to use FAA approved Portable Oxygen Concentrators (POC) during flight, with advance notice to JetBlue and an accompanying physician’s medical documentation confirming the need for a POC. Customers must meet all JetBlue requirements associated with carriage of this device as found in Chapter 8 of the SOM. In JetBlue’s Mint seating, the customer will forfeit the use of the reclining feature in order to accommodate the use of the POC during flight. Stowage of the POC during takeoff and landing will be required in the overhead compartment to comply with FAA requirements as the footwell area will not accommodate storage.

    • Since meals are included in the Mint Experience, is there an option for Mint customers with special dietary restrictions?

      Yes. Mint customers with special dietary requests may choose from menus that feature Kosher (except flights that depart from the Caribbean and Costa Rica), Vegetarian/Vegan, Low Calorie, Gluten-free and a ‘Plane Eats’ meal. All special meal requests must be ordered at least 24 hours before departure. Meal requests can be made during the online booking process by checking the ‘add a special request for this traveler’ box, or by calling 1-800-JETBLUE. 

    • Which flight times are associated with which Mint menus?

       

      Customers on flights departing before 9:46AM are served Breakfast. Flights departing 9:47AM - 7PM (and westbound flights departing 7PM & later) are served Lunch/Dinner, while eastbound-only flights departing 7PM & later are served Shuteye menu. All flights offer our special menus (Kosher, Vegetarian/Vegan, Low Calorie, Gluten-Free, Plane Eats).

    • Why are no pets allowed in Mint?

      The configuration of the Mint seats prevents any under-seat stowage which includes pet carriers.

    • Are children allowed in Mint seats?

      Yes, there are no age restrictions for Mint.

    • Are lap infants allowed in Mint?

      Yes, any Mint customer may travel with one lap infant under the age of 2.

    • Can unaccompanied minors be booked in the Mint seats?

      No, unaccompanied minors (UNMRs) are not allowed in Mint.

    • Important Information About Mint Seats

      For your child’s safety and comfort, please be aware of the following details regarding our Mint seats:

      • Mint seats are equipped with shoulder straps to enhance security.
      • It’s essential that both the shoulder straps and seatbelt fit your child properly.
      • If the seatbelt does not fit correctly, you and your child may be asked to move to a different seat to ensure optimal safety.

    • Can I request to move to Mint at the airport?

      Yes. If you already booked a Main seat (whether it was with cash, points or Cash + Points), you can move to Mint (if there’s space available) at the airport for an additional fee. 

    • Can I request to move to Mint at the check-in desk?

      Yes, Moves to Mint can be requested at the check-in desk, gate, or JustAsk Desk at the departure airport of the Mint flight and will be honored on a first-come, first-served basis, subject to availability. 

    • Can I redeem TrueBlue points to move to Mint at the airport?

      No. TrueBlue points can only be redeemed for new Mint bookings. They can’t be used to move from a Main seat to Mint.  

    • Will I receive bonus or extra TrueBlue points if I pay to move to Mint at the airport?

      No, moving to Mint at the airport will not earn you any additional TrueBlue points, and you will still earn TrueBlue points based on the fare that you booked.

    • Will I receive a refund if I paid for checked bags and then moved to Mint at the gate or JustAsk desk?

      No, your checked bag allowance is based on the original fare purchased, Mosaic status &/or JetBlue cardmember status.  

    • Do I get all of the benefits of the Mint fare when I move to Mint at the gate or JustAsk desk?

      No, moving to Mint at the gate or JustAsk desk does not come with free priority security, 2 free checked bags, or any bonus TrueBlue points.

Have questions about our fares?

We have answers.

  • Is there a fee for same-day switches?

    Same-day switches (as well as same-day standby) are subject to a $100 fee (but no difference in fare), with the following exceptions: 

    • If you booked a Flex fare, you can make same-day switches for free. (That’s no fee, no difference in fare.)

    To qualify as a same-day switch:

    • The booking must be changed on the calendar day you were scheduled to depart, beginning at midnight in the time zone of the departure airport. Or, if you’re a Mosaic member, same-day switches can be made up to 24 hours before your scheduled departure.
    • You must be switching to a different departure time (earlier or later) on the same day as the original departure.
    • The origin and destination cities must remain the same, but you can change airports within the same city (so, for instance LGA to JFK).

    Of course, if the difference in fare is less than $100 (and you booked a standard fare option), you have the option of cancelling your flight and making a new booking to avoid paying the same-day switch fee.

  • My travel plans may change. Which is the most flexible fare?

    Flex fares offer the most flexibility. In addition to no change or cancellation fees, you get the extra perk of same-day switches and same-day standby without paying a difference in fare.

    Main, EvenMore, and Mint fares do not have change or cancellation fees but a fare difference may apply, and changes are subject to fare rules applicable on date of change. 

    Base fares can be changed or cancelled for a fee of $150 per person.

    You can make same-day switches and or fly same-day standby if the difference in fare is more than $100. 

    For cancellations, funds will be in the form of a JetBlue travel credit, valid for 12 months from original ticketing date. 

    You also have the option to purchase one of our Flex fares, to have funds credited back to original form of payment.

  • Can I bring a carry-on bag with Main Base?

    All fares (including Main Base) include a carry-on bag that fits in the overhead bin (space permitting), along with a personal item that fits under the seat in front of you.

  • Can I change a Base fare booking?

    Yes, Base fares may be changed or cancelled for a $150 fee.

  • Do Mosaic benefits apply to Main Base fares?

    As a valued Mosaic member, you (and other eligible travelers completing the same itinerary) will still get up to 2 checked bags for free, a carry-on bag and enjoy early boarding with all fares, including Main Base. You’ll also still enjoy your Mosaic Signature Perks and Perks You Pick®.    Mosaics considering a Main Base fare need to be aware of the following:  

    • Changes and cancellations to Main Base bookings are subject to a fee of $150 (fare difference may apply), even with Mosaic status.     
    • Same day switches or same day standby can be made for a $100 fee.
    • Main Base customers (including Mosaic members) may select seats at any time for a fee. If you don’t, your seats will be assigned before departure. 
    • You’ll earn 1 TrueBlue point per dollar spent—or 2 points if you booked on jetblue.com or the JetBlue app—plus your usual Mosaic (and JetBlue card, if you have one) bonus.

  • Can families traveling together be seated together with Main Base?

    If you’re traveling with others and want to sit together, we recommend booking a fare that offers free advance seat selection, like Main or Main Flex. You may select seats for Main Base fares at any time for a fee. If you don’t, your seats will be assigned before departure.

  • Can I bring a pet with a Main Base fare?

    Yes. You may add a pet (space permitting) to a Main Base booking. Keep in mind you can bring a carry-on bag and your approved pet carrier counts as your personal item. 

  • Can I purchase an EvenMore® seat with a Main Base fare?

    Absolutely! If an EvenMore® seat is available, you will be given the option during seat selection.

  • I bought Main Base but want to board earlier. Is that possible?

    Yes. If an EvenMore® seat is available, you can purchase it to enjoy Group 2 boarding. As a reminder, Mosaic members always enjoy Mosaic boarding, even when traveling on a Main Base fare.

  • Can I purchase a Main Base fare with TrueBlue points?

    Yes. TrueBlue points can be redeemed toward Main Base fares.

  • Do any of the fares include free checked bags?

    Yes. Mint includes 2 checked bags. And, on all transatlantic flights and select international routes, Main, Main Flex, EvenMore, and EvenMore Flex also include 1 checked bag.    Mosaic members (and eligible travel companions completing the same itinerary) get up to 2 checked bags, and JetBlue Plus and JetBlue Business Cardmembers (and up to 3 eligible travel companions completing the same itinerary) get 1 free checked bag. 

  • Are checked bag fees refundable?

    Checked bag fees (as well as other extras like EvenMore® seat selection, pets, etc.) are refundable to the original form of payment, if the booking is canceled prior to the scheduled departure. Checked bag fees are not refundable if you travel on the flight but end up not checking the bag(s) you paid for.

Refunds

When you travel with us you have options. Our refundable and nonrefundable fares give you additional flexibility to your flights and any travel credits you earn with us are in good hands.

Cancellations within 24 hours

If your travel was booked seven days or more prior to the scheduled departure date, you have 24 hours from the time the booking was made to cancel your reservation without being charged a cancellation fee. The entire booking must be cancelled to qualify (not applicable for JetBlue Vacations reservations).

Change or cancel your flight

Nonrefundable fare policy

For JetBlue nonrefundable fares, cancellations can be made prior to the scheduled departure. A per person cancellation fee may apply depending on the fare option selected. Any remaining balance will be in the form of a JetBlue travel credit, valid for 12 months from original ticketing date.

No show or missed flight

If an eligible nonrefundable reservation is not changed or cancelled prior to scheduled departure, all money associated with the reservation will be forfeited. This includes extra ancillary purchases such as EvenMore® seat selection, priority security, additional checked bag fees, etc.

See JetBlue travel credit info

Mixed fare policy

Where one leg of a fare is ticketed as a refundable fare and another leg of a fare is ticketed as a non-refundable fare, the applicable refund and cancellation policies for refundable fares will apply only to the refundable portion and the applicable refund and cancellation policies for the non-refundable fare will apply to the non-refundable portion.

Refundable fare policy (Flex fares)

For JetBlue refundable fares (Main Flex, EvenMore Flex, and Mint Flex), cancellations are permitted any time prior to the scheduled departure for a full refund to the original form of payment. Refundable fares may not be available on all flights.

If an eligible refundable reservation is not changed or cancelled prior to the scheduled departure, all money associated with the reservation will be in the form of a JetBlue travel credit, valid for 12 months from original ticketing date, and may be applied toward future travel.

Partially used reservation

If you wish to cancel any remaining travel after a portion of your refundable trip has been taken, your refund amount will equal the one-way fare for the portion of the trip cancelled. This also applies if a portion of your travel is not operated as scheduled by JetBlue.

No show or missed flight

Refundable fares must be changed or cancelled prior to the scheduled departure time in order to receive a refund to the original form of payment. If a flight is missed, the money associated with the reservation will be in the form of a JetBlue travel credit, valid for 12 months from original ticketing date, and may be applied toward future travel.

See JetBlue travel credit info

Help Centre

Accessibility Guidelines

We’re here to help make your travel experience safe and smooth.

Qualified individual with a disability

An individual with a disability is any individual who has a physical or cognitive, intellectual, or developmental disability that, on a permanent or temporary basis, substantially limits one or more major life activities. Examples include, but are not limited to: hearing, seeing, breathing, walking, speaking.

Customer accessibility

JetBlue accommodates customers with disabilities consistent with its obligations under 14 C.F.R. Part 382. We will not refuse transportation to anyone on the basis of disability. 

To learn more about JetBlue’s travel guidelines, visit our Accessibility Assistance page or Contract of Carriage. Our Contract of Carriage is also available at any JetBlue airport ticket counter.

Airlines for America Passenger Accessibility Commitment

JetBlue has signed on to the Airlines For America Passenger Accessibility Commitment and is proud to be among the member airlines committed to further improving the experience for customers with disabilities across the industry and to ensuring every customer feels welcome, comfortable, and valued.

Airline Passengers with Disabilities Bill of Rights

The Airline Passengers with Disabilities Bill of Rights ensures the fundamental rights of air travelers with disabilities under the Air Carrier Access Act.

Adding Bags and Paying Fees

Have a fare that doesn’t include a bag? No problem! You can add checked bags to your reservation at any time leading up to your flight’s departure.

Adding bags to your reservation

  • After making your booking

    Bags can be added to your reservation from our website by using the Manage Trips option on jetblue.com. Just go to your Extras and you can add a bag and pay any applicable fees at any time leading up to your departure.

    You can also contact us if you need assistance with adding bags to your reservation.

  • During check-in

    You can indicate how many bags you’re bringing and pay the applicable fees during the check-in process within 24 hours of your flight’s departure.

    For your convenience, we recommend checking in online or at one of our airport kiosks. During the check-in timeframe, there is no difference in fees whether adding your bags online, at a kiosk or at the check-in counter.

    If you check in online, drop your bags at the bag drop location when you arrive at the airport. A crewmember will scan your boarding document to confirm your payment, verify the bag count and weight, and print your bag tag(s).

    Overweight and oversized baggage fees may apply.

    See checked bag fees

     

Adding checked bags

  • How do I add and pay for checked bags?

    Add your first or second bag during booking or anytime before the check-in window opens. If you would like to check a third bag (or more), it must be checked at the airport. If you would like to check multiple bags, follow this guide:

    • Check your first bag: Online during booking or at airport kiosk during check in.
    • Check your second bag: Only available through the Manage Trips tool or at the airport kiosk.
    • Check your third and any additional bags: At airport only. Please check in at ticket counter.

  • How do I add and pay for checked bags?

    • Checked bags can’t be removed from a reservation.
    • Checked bag fees are only refundable if your entire booking is cancelled prior to scheduled departure.

Paying for checked bags

Customers may purchase up to two pre-paid checked bags per person (see fare options to confirm). If you would like to check a third bag, it must be checked at the airport.

  • Pay for first bag: Online or at airport kiosk during check in.
  • Pay for second bag: Only available through the Manage Trips tool.
  • Pay for third bag: At airport only. Please check in at ticket counter.

 

  • How do I pay for an overweight bag?

    Overweight bags must be weighed and paid for at the airport ticket counter. Allow for extra time at the airport for check-in.

  • Can I use my JetBlue travel credit to pay for a bag?

    JetBlue travel credits cannot be applied to the following extras:

    • Checked bags 
    • Same-day switches 
    • Seat selection fees for Main Base, EvenMore®, extra legroom, priority security, pets, unaccompanied minors, service fees (including phone and GDS bookings and changes), etc. Learn more by visiting JetBlue Travel Credits.

  • What bag fees can I expect to pay for a roundtrip flight?

    • If you are checking bags on a roundtrip itinerary, you can expect to pay bag fees each way. 
    • Save up to $10 each on your first 2 checked bags when you add them before check-in (at least 24 hours before departure). Learn more about checked bag fees.

  • I’m trying to pay for checked bags, but the payment screen gets stuck.

    We’re sorry for any inconvenience. Please contact us for assistance.

Alcohol Guidelines

Federal regulations

Always check the TSA website for the most current regulations. 

You are allowed to bring one quart-sized bag of liquids or gels through the security checkpoint. These are limited to travel-sized containers that must be 3.4 ounces or less per item. With the exception of medications, any amount of liquid greater than 3.4 ounces, including alcohol, must be packed in your checked bags. 

Alcoholic beverages being carried on board in containers greater than 3.4 ounces must be purchased in the secure boarding area (after going through the security checkpoint). However, JetBlue will not be liable for damage, loss or spoilage of these items. 

You may bring wine, champagne or beer on a flight as long as it is in an unopened container. You are not allowed to consume your own alcohol while on board.

Alcohol in checked or carry-on bags

Domestic flights

Alcohol with less than 24 percent alcohol by volume (ABV), or 48 proof (like most wine and beer)

Checked bag: Yes

There is no quantity limit as long as what you bring remains within the allowable size and weight limited for checked bags.

Carry-on: Yes

You are limited to containers of 3.4 ounces or less that can fit comfortably in one quart-sized, clear, zip-top bag per the TSA’s 3-1-1 directive. Please remember, one bag per customer.

Alcohol between 24-70 percent ABV, or 48-140 proof

Checked bag: Yes Alcohol must be in unopened retail packaging and you are limited to five liters (1.3 gallons) per customer.

Carry-on: Yes You are limited to containers of 3.4 ounces or less that can fit comfortably in one quart-sized, clear, zip-top bag per the TSA’s 3-1-1 directive. Please remember, one bag per customer.

Alcohol over 70 percent ABV, or over 140 proof

Checked bag: No Carry-on: No

Alcoholic beverages with more than 70 percent or 140 proof alcohol content, including 95 percent grain alcohol and 150 proof rum, are not permitted in checked or carry-on bags.

International flights

U.S. Customs and Border Protection has the most up-to-date alcohol guidelines for international flights.

US Customs & Border Protection

Need help?

Bag Claims

We’ll do everything we can to help you if your bags are damaged, delayed or lost. Even if you have left something behind on your flight, we have steps to help you recover it.

Making a bag claim

Delayed or damaged bags

JetBlue offers online baggage claim reports so you can quickly file a claim without stopping at a Baggage Service Office. Here’s how:

  • File your claim using the online claim form within four hours of landing.
  • Check your claim by logging in.

0

File an online bag claim

Central Baggage Services

Central Baggage Services may be contacted at 1-866-538-5438 if additional assistance is needed for bag claims.

See additional bag info

  • What should I do if I cannot complete my claim?

    If you get an error message while creating a claim and are still at the airport, please complete your claim with a JetBlue crewmember. Otherwise, you can call Central Baggage Services at 1-866-538-5438 for assistance.

  • What happens once I’ve created my claim?

    Once your report is created, a worldwide electronic search is initiated that matches your bag claim number, bag type, color and contents with similar bags. This is in addition to manual searches conducted by JetBlue crewmembers.

  • Can my delayed bag be shipped to me?

    Yes. You will have the option to pick up your delayed bag at the airport, or have it shipped to either a permanent or temporary address. Your bag will be delivered to the address listed on your claim. You can update the address listed on your claim via the customer portal online up until the point the delivery driver has picked up your bag.

  • When will my bag be delivered?

    In U.S. cities, you will receive an email with your bag delivery information, including a picture of the driver and the vehicle delivering your bag. The delivery service contact information will also be included if you choose to contact your delivery driver directly. Bags are not delivered after 10pm except to hotels and military bases.

  • What should I do about replacing certain contents which I need immediately?

    Please purchase any essential and reasonable items you need while your baggage is delayed (for example: necessary clothing and toiletries). It is very important to retain all original paid receipts for replaced items. Your online claim includes a customer portal where you can upload receipts for review. Simply log in to the portal to submit your receipts.

  • What if my bag is damaged upon delivery?

    If your bag was damaged while it was delayed, please call our Central Baggage Services at 1-866-538-5438 to report the damage within four hours of delivery. You will be asked to provide photo documentation of the damage.

Lost and found

We will make every effort to return articles found onboard a JetBlue aircraft or in a JetBlue terminal as quickly as possible.

Report a lost item

  • I have left something on the plane. What do I do now?

    If you realize that you left something on the plane, please let a crewmember at the airport know immediately. If you have already left the airport, please file a lost and found report online.

    Report a lost item

  • What information do I need to provide when filing a report online?

    If possible, gather the descriptive information about the item(s) prior to filling out the form, and be sure to include any and all identifiable details to help in our search. Please enter the information as it appears on the form. Also, be sure to include the flight date and flight number reflecting when your item(s) was lost.

    You may also provide additional information in the “Help us find your item-what makes it unique?” field. We also ask that you file your report as soon as you notice the item was left behind.

  • I did not receive the confirmation email for my report. What should I do?

    If you did not receive a confirmation email after submitting your report online, please check your spam/junk folder. The email comes from noreply@lostandfound.aero.

  • Will someone keep me updated on the status of the search for my item?

    Once the report has been completed, you will receive an email confirmation. If we find an item that matches the description of the lost item, we will send you an email notification with further instructions. We will continue to search for your item for up to 30 days and will update you via email.

  • How long will you continue to search for my item?

    We will thoroughly search for your item for approximately 30 days from the date the item is reported missing. If we are unable to find your item, we will send an email notification informing you that we have been unsuccessful in our efforts.

  • If my item is located, how do I have it returned to me?

    We will send you an email with a link to the shipping checkout page. You will be able to verify your shipping information and provide credit card information for shipping charges.

  • What happens to all official documents or government-issued IDs if the owner is not located?

    Passports will be sent to the US Department of State, Passport Services, and Consular Lost/Stolen Passport Section. All other personal documents are destroyed.

  • What do I do if my contact information has changed?

    On the email you received when you opened your report, there is a link to the Lost and Found page. Please log into the page with your last name and report number to edit your report. You can only edit within the first few days.

Need help?

Bag Limitations for Select Markets

Your bags won’t have any problems getting their passport stamped, but boxes and bins may not be cleared for take-off if you are flying internationally.

Checked bag exceptions for select markets

Boxes and plastic bins are not accepted on international flights. See our Checked Bag Fees section in Bag Info for a complete list of fees. For information on bikes and surfboards, please visit Sports Gear.

Ecuador

For flights to/from Ecuador:

  • Each customer is allowed to check a maximum of 2 bags. 
  • Each checked bag cannot exceed 62 inches (157.48 cm) in overall dimensions. Bag dimensions are determined by adding the length, width, and height together.
  • Overweight bag fees apply to bags weighing over 50 pounds (22.73 kg).
  • Bags that exceed 70 pounds (31.75 kg) are not accepted. 
The Dominican Republic

For flights to/from the Dominican Republic:

  • Each checked bag cannot exceed 62 inches (157.48 cm) in overall dimensions. Bag dimensions are determined by adding the length, width, and height together.
  • A 3rd checked bag (or more) can only be added at the airport or during check in. Excess bag fees apply.
  • Overweight bag fees apply to bags weighing over 50 pounds (22.73 kg).
  • Bags that exceed 70 pounds (31.75 kg) are not accepted.
Guyana, Haiti, and Trinidad & Tobago

For flights to/from Guyana, Haiti, and Trinidad & Tobago:

  • Each checked bag cannot exceed 62 inches (157.48 cm) in overall dimensions. Bag dimensions are determined by adding the length, width, and height together.
  • A 3rd checked bag (or more) can only be added at the airport or during check in. Excess bag fees apply.
  • Overweight bag fees apply to bags weighing over 50 pounds (22.73 kg).
  • Bags that exceed 70 pounds (31.75 kg) are not accepted.
  • From Nov 16 - Jan 15, each customer may check a maximum of 2 bags.
U.K./Europe

For flights to/from the U.K./Europe:

  • The fee for a 3rd (or more) checked bag is $200/£150/€185. 
  • A 3rd checked bag (or more) can only be added at the airport or during check in.
  • Bags that exceed 70 pounds (31.75 kg) are not accepted.

Additional exceptions and limitations

Boxes and televisions Cardboard boxes and plastic bins are allowed when traveling within the U.S., but are not allowed on any international flight. When transporting a television within the U.S., the television must be packaged in its original, factory-sealed box.

Humanitarian organizations Humanitarian organizations transporting supplies are subject to the same bag allowances and guidelines as other customers.

Military personnel U.S. military personnel, their dependents, and family members have separate bag exceptions. Learn more about military bag exceptions.

Need help?

Batteries

Batteries not included, but you can bring your own.

Batteries for Electronic Devices

Batteries for electronic devices

Due to the possibility of a fire, all batteries for electronic devices must be packed in the original retail package or wrapped in insulating tape to prevent loose batteries from coming into contact with metal objects, causing a short circuit.

Please note: Lithium batteries should be packed in carry-on bags, not checked bags, to protect the battery from getting crushed or punctured during the flight.

Lithium Batteries in Devices

JetBlue will accept consumer electronic and medical devices (watches, calculators, cameras, cellular phones, laptop computers, camcorders, hearing aids, etc.) containing lithium cells or batteries when carried by customers or crewmembers in carry-on bags (with the exception of mobility devices, which can be checked in). Portable electronic devices in checked bags must be turned off and prevented from accidental activation. However, JetBlue prefers that all Lithium powered devices or at least their batteries, are transported in the cabin.

Spare lithium cells and batteries for these devices will only be accepted in carry-on bags and are prohibited in checked bags. When a carry-on bag is checked at the gate or at planeside, all spare lithium batteries must be removed from the bag and kept with the customer in the aircraft cabin.

See 0 for a complete list of acceptable batteries and how to properly pack them.

  • What are lithium batteries?

    Lithium batteries power many everyday electronic devices, including cell phones, laptops, tablets, smart watches, cameras, wireless headphones, and power banks.

  • What is thermal runaway?

    In rare cases, a lithium battery can overheat due to damage, a defect, or other factors. This can lead to a process called thermal runaway, where the battery rapidly generates heat and may produce smoke, fire, or intense flames.

  • What should I do if my device overheats?

    If your electronic device or lithium battery becomes unusually hot or begins to smoke during your flight, notify a JetBlue crewmember immediately. Crewmembers are trained to respond quickly and have specialized equipment and procedures to safely manage lithium battery incidents.

Battery requirements on flights

JetBlue accepts assistive devices with lithium ion, spillable, and non-spillable battery types, with restrictions.

  • Battery Type

    Description Checked Carry-on

  • Lithium-ion/polymer batteries (rechargeable) 

    A rechargeable battery that uses lithium ions to store energy and create a charge. Yes, with restrictions

    Yes. Batteries must be marked/documented as meeting UN 38.3 testing and must not be more than 100 wh.

    For mobility aids we will accept one battery up to 300 wh with one spare or two batteries up to 160 whand two spares. 

  • Spillable Batteries 

    A spillable battery is a wet-cell battery or a battery that lacks manufacturer labeling. It is identified by caps that open to give access to the cells.

  • What do I need to know about battery documentation, labels, and inspections?

    Bring copies of any documentation for the batteries you wish to carry.  This can usually be found in the user manual of the device.

    • All batteries being used in medical devices must have a clear, readable manufacturer label and meet federal regulation requirements.  
    • Batteries may be inspected for airworthiness at the airport.  
    • For lithium batteries, customer must be aware of the battery chemistry (ion vs. metal) as well as the capacity and watt hours (wh). 

  • What do I need to know about Lithium-ion battery types/requirements/power limits?

    • Mobility devices that have enclosed, installed, protected lithium-ion batteries, are allowed as checked items on JetBlue flights, but if possible, batteries should be removed and carried in the cabin where they are accessible in case of thermal events. 
    • For mobility devices that do not have a protective housing for the lithium-ion battery, the battery must be removed. The battery terminals must be protected from short-circuiting. JetBlue will notify the pilot of the battery location in the cabin.
    • Lithium-ion battery size is limited to 300-watt hours (Wh) or 25 grams equivalent lithium content (ELC). 
    • Lithium-ion batteries over this Wh limit are never allowed on JetBlue flights.  
      • One (1) spare battery not exceeding 300 Wh or two (2) spare batteries not exceeding 160 Wh (13.5 grams ELC) each may be carried in carry-on baggage. 
    • Lithium metal (non-rechargeable lithium) batteries are forbidden with these devices.
    • Non-mobility devices (i.e., portable electronic devices) battery limit is 100 wh for lithium ion, or 2 grams lithium metal content. 

  • How do I calculate how much battery power I need for the duration of my flight?

    • JetBlue requires that you travel with enough power to cover 150% of the travel time of your trip. This requirement is intended to cover your trip and any delays that may arise during your travels.  
      • For example, if your flight is 6 hours long, we require that you must travel with battery power to cover 9 hours of use.  

Power Banks

  • No more than two power banks allowed per customer
  • Power banks are not permitted in checked baggage or overhead compartments
  • Power banks must not be charged using in seat power during flight
  • Power banks should not be used to charge or power other devices during flight

Boarding Procedures

JetBlue welcomes you aboard your next flight with us! If you have questions about the boarding process, you can find the answers here.

Group boarding

JetBlue boards by boarding group. We recently implemented numeric boarding groups (Groups 1–8), to make boarding more intuitive, consistent,  easier to follow along at the gate—and easier to hear and understand during boarding announcements. Please see below for more details.  

Your boarding group is noted on your boarding pass above or next to your seat number. Customers are encouraged to remain comfortably seated until their group is called for boarding. If you don’t see a group number on your boarding pass, please see a gate crewmember.  

Mosaic members, and Mint® and EvenMore® customers enjoy priority boarding. Customers on the same reservation and checked in together will be assigned to the same boarding group. Customers on the same reservation who check in separately are not guaranteed the same boarding group. This includes Mosaic members. 

Boarding order

  • Pre-boarding: For customers with disabilities 
  • Group 1: Mosaic 3 & 4 members, Mint customers 
  • Group 2: Mosaic 1 & 2 members, EvenMore® customers 
  • Group 3: JetBlue Premier Card cardmembers, JetBlue Business Card cardmembers, Early Boarding Perks You Pick® (excluding Main Base fares) 
  • Courtesy Boarding: Active military, Customers traveling with car seats or strollers 
  • Group 4: JetBlue Vacations customers, Corporate Perks customers, and general boarding based on seat location 
  • Groups 5 - 8: General boarding based on seat location  

Note: Unaccompanied minors will be escorted to the aircraft and will board last.

Boarding cut-off times

Any customer not on board, even if they have checked in and received a boarding pass, may be denied boarding and will not be entitled to denied boarding compensation.

Domestic flights

Each customer must be on board the aircraft at least 15 minutes prior to departure.

International flights

Each customer must be on board the aircraft at least 20 minutes prior to departure.

Cancellations from Colombia

Want to stay in Colombia a little longer? We don’t blame you.

Requesting a refund

  • Ticket Rescind

    When you purchase a ticket through any reservation channel from the point of sale in Colombia, you can cancel the trip and request a refund of your money, as long as you notify JetBlue at least twenty-four (24) hours in advance of your scheduled departure time. JetBlue may retain up to 10% of the fare, excluding fees, taxes and administrative fees. This rule is not applicable to promotional or non-refundable fares.

  • Withdrawal Rights

    When you buy a ticket through online or phone reservation channels from the point of sale in Colombia, you can withdraw and request a refund of your money within five (5) business days after making your purchase without discounts or withholdings, except those that by legal provision are not reimbursable. The refund must be requested before checking in for your scheduled flight.

If you would like to exercise this right, contact JetBlue. 

Please provide the following:

  • Confirmation number
  • Flight number
  • Date(s) of travel
  • Customer information (e.g., name, DOB, contact information, etc.)

Cargo on JetBlue

With a modern fleet of Airbus A220s, A320s and A321s, our partnership with Aeronex Cargo, and access to cargo handling terminals at a growing number of U.S. and international airports, we offer 1-day, 2-day and 3-day service for freight forwarders, small and large companies, and organizations.

What we ship.

From aircraft parts to zinnias—we’ll get your shipments where they need to be, when they need to be.

While we made headlines for shipping life-saving ventilators, PPE and plasma during the pandemic (over 217 tons), you can trust us to treat all shipments—whether it’s newspapers or textbooks, fresh-cut flowers or freshly-caught seafood—with the same sense of urgency and care. 

We can handle your cargo.

Carry-On Bags

JetBlue lets you bring one carry-on and one personal item on all fares, with no weight limit as long as you can lift it.

General Info

All fares

Each customer is allowed one carry-on bag and one personal item (purse, small backpack, briefcase, laptop, etc.). Carry-on bags may be stowed in the overhead bin or underneath the seat in front of you, while personal items must be placed beneath the seat in front of you. Closets for hanging garment bags are not available on our planes.

Carry-on bags are the sole responsibility of the customer and JetBlue will assume no liability for property carried on board.

Size and weight restrictions

Size restrictions

Overhead bin: Including wheels and handles, carry-on bags must not exceed 22” L (55.88 cm) x 14” W (35.56 cm) x 9” H (22.86 cm).

Under the seat: Personal items must not exceed 17” L (43.2 cm) x 13” W (33 cm) x 8” H (20.32 cm).

Weight restrictions

 

JetBlue does not currently have a weight restriction for carry-on bags. Please make sure items can be lifted into the overhead bin if assistance is not available and the item does not fit beneath the seat.

Carry-on regulations

In addition to one carry-on bag and one personal item, FAA/TSA regulations allow the following items, which do not count towards the carry-on limit:

  • Assistive devices
  • Duty-free items in a reasonable and limited amount
  • One diaper bag for lap infants
  • Special items (coat, umbrella, infant car seat, etc.)

You may find more information about prohibited items by visiting TSA’s website. Keep in mind that TSA regulations and enforcement may change without notice.

Looking to check bags? See how much it will cost.

Bag Calculator

Carry-on FAQ

  • My trip has both a JetBlue flight and an American flight. What is my bag allowance and do I get a carry-on bag?

    • Please check your confirmation email to learn about your bag allowance for your trip. 
    • In general, the first airline on your first leg, each way, determines the bag rules as you check your bags. So be sure to look up that airline’s bag allowances and fees. This applies to carry-ons and checked bags.  
    • It is a good idea to check on both airlines’ baggage limits, especially if you are traveling internationally.

  • I can’t tell if my added bag is in my reservation.

    To check to see if you added your bag successfully to your booking, complete these steps:  

    1. Log into Manage Trips. 
    2. Choose your upcoming trip. 
    3. Scroll down to Traveler. Click the dropdown.  
    4. Choose Travelers Details. 
    5. Click Details to see if you previously added a bag. 

  • I’m not able to add a checked bag on the app.

    To add up to two checked bags on the JetBlue app, complete these steps:

    • Tap your upcoming trip. 
    • Tap the blue Manage Trips banner to open the dropdown. 
    • Tap the Bags & extras link to add a bag. 

    Still not working? Check to be sure you have a JetBlue flight, not a partner airline flight. Keep in mind you can only check in for JetBlue flights with JetBlue.

  • How do I pay for a friend or relative’s bag if I’m not traveling?

    The credit card used to pay for this bag does not need to match the credit card used to pay for the trip. Follow these steps: 

    • Visit Manage Trips. Be sure you have the Confirmation Code or Ticket Number ready, plus the traveler’s last name.  
    • Choose the upcoming trip. 
    • On Desktop, click Bags & extras.  
    • On Mobile, tap the blue Manage Trips banner to open the dropdown and click Bags & extras. 
    • Follow the steps to add a bag.  

  • Why are checked bags not available for this leg of the trip?

    There are a few common reasons why checked bags are not available for purchase: 

    • This leg of your flight may be on another airline. You can only purchase a checked bag from JetBlue if you are booked on a JetBlue-operated flight. You may need to check in with a different airline. 
    • There is a bag check limit as you book the flight. Customers can purchase up to 2 bags during booking. You can only purchase a third bag (or more) at the airport.

  • My flight was switched. Do I have to check my bags again or will they be put on the new flight? Will I be charged again?

    • If you decide to switch flights from one JetBlue flight to another JetBlue flight, we refund your bag fees to your original form of payment and recollect payment for the new flight. 
    • If JetBlue changes you to a different flight, from one JetBlue flight to another JetBlue flight, we can reassign your “extras” purchased like bags or seats, without an increase or decrease in cost. 
    • If JetBlue assigns you a flight on a different airline, you will be required to pay for your bags again. You will then need to request a refund from JetBlue.  

  • I prepaid my bags, but now I am not bringing them. How do I get a refund?

    • Once bags are prepaid, we cannot remove them from your reservation.  
    • In order to refund the bag fees, the entire roundtrip reservation would need to be cancelled. If you cancel your reservation, keep in mind change/cancel fees may apply and there may be a fare difference when you rebook.

  • Can I get a refund on my upgraded seat(s) and other extras?

    • Extras are non-refundable. Once they are purchased, they cannot be removed from your reservation.  
    • In order to return the fees, the entire reservation would need to be cancelled. If you cancel your reservation, keep in mind change/cancel fees may apply and there may be a fare difference when you rebook.

Cremated Remains

Cremated human remains must be transported in a crematory container (urn) and will only be accepted as a carry-on item. Urns will not count as part of the customer’s personal item or carry-on item baggage requirements. The urn must fit completely stowed under the seat in front of the customer or in an overhead bin. The urn may not be held by a customer for taxi, takeoff & landing.

Traveling Requirements

In order to travel with cremated remains, customers must provide the following documentation to a JetBlue crewmember at the airport ticket counter.

  • A certified copy of the death certificate.
  • An original/certified document or letter from the funeral director or crematorium that performed the cremation.

Customers traveling to international destinations are solely responsible for obtaining all necessary documentation and otherwise complying with the laws of the destination country regarding the entry and clearance of cremated remains prior to travel.

Security Screening

Upon entry through the TSA security checkpoint, the urn will be sent through the screening device.  If the urn cannot be cleared by TSA, it will not be permitted onboard the aircraft.  TSA cannot state for certain whether particular urns can be successfully screened; however, they recommend you purchase a temporary or permanent urn made of a lighter weight material, such as cardboard, wood or plastic for travel. 

Pet Remains

JetBlue does not accept pet remains, cremated or otherwise.

Earning TrueBlue Points

Earn TrueBlue points effortlessly by flying JetBlue, booking with our partners, or using JetBlue credit cards—every dollar spent gets you closer to your next adventure!

Earning Points

How are TrueBlue points calculated?

TrueBlue points are earned by purchasing qualifying services or products from JetBlue, JetBlue Vacations, TrueBlue Travel, or with a JetBlue co-branded credit card.

Points earned on JetBlue are based on the fare paid for JetBlue-operated flights and eligible fees, less taxes and non-eligible fees. You earn 3 points for every dollar spent except on Base fares where you earn 1 point for every dollar spent. Members who book on jetblue.com can earn an extra 3 bonus points per dollar spent except on Base fares where you earn 1 point for every dollar spent, as follows:

  • Members who have purchased a Main, Main Flex, EvenMore®, EvenMore® Flex, Mint or Mint Flex fare on jetblue.com can earn an extra 3 bonus points per dollar for a total of 6 points per dollar spent.
  • Members who have purchased a Main Base or EvenMore® Base fare on jetblue.com can earn an extra bonus point per dollar for a total of 2 points per dollar spent.

Learn more about earning points.

Is there a benefit to booking travel at jetblue.com or on the JetBlue app?

Absolutely! If you book on jetblue.com or the JetBlue app, you earn double points on your purchase—so you’d get a total of 6 points per eligible dollar spent on Main, Main Flex, EvenMore®, EvenMore® Flex, Mint or Mint Flex fares (instead of 3 points), and 2 points per dollar for Main Base and EvenMore® Base fares (instead of 1 point).  No bonus points are earned if JetBlue-operated flights are booked by phone or otherwise.

Can I earn TrueBlue points by means other than flying on JetBlue?

Rack up points on everyday purchases with our travel, retail and credit card partners. Explore all our TrueBlue partners so you can more quickly earn points towards your next award flight. To find out more about earning TrueBlue points on other JetBlue travel products and services powered by our travel suppliers, check out JetBlue Vacations and TrueBlue Travel.

Can I earn points for a JetBlue flight I purchase for someone else?

No. You cannot earn points for flights purchased for another traveler in the TrueBlue program.

I forgot to add my TrueBlue number when I booked. Is there a way to get points for a flight I’ve already completed?

Yes. When signed in to your TrueBlue account, click the Request missing points link. You are able to request points for a JetBlue-operated flight you completed within 12 months of that flight. Length of time to request points for qualifying TrueBlue partner airlines may vary. Refer to the page of the specific TrueBlue partner airline for more details: Cape Air, Etihad Airways, Hawaiian Airlines, Icelandair, Qatar Airways, Silver Airways, Singapore Airlines, Condor, United Airlines, and South African Airways.

 

Point Eligibility

What flights are eligible to earn TrueBlue points?

All JetBlue-operated flights purchased on jetblue.com are eligible to earn TrueBlue points. You can also earn TrueBlue points on qualifying flights operated by our loyalty partners. To receive TrueBlue points on a flight operated by a TrueBlue partner airline, you must purchase an eligible fare and enter your TrueBlue number when booking your flight online. For more information on redeeming TrueBlue points, please read more here. 

 

FAQ

  • Why hasn’t my account changed to show the new points I just earned?

    The points that you have just earned can take up to 14 days after completed travel to post to your account. For non-air Paisly products, please allow up to 30 days after completed travel for points to post to your account.

Electronic Cigarettes

Yes, you can travel with e-cigs and similar devices. However, there are restrictions in place to ensure the safety of our customers and crewmembers.

Electronic Cigarettes and Vaping Devices

Electronic cigarettes (e-cigarettes), vaporizers, vape pens, atomizers, and electronic nicotine delivery systems and spare batteries may only be carried in the aircraft cabin (in carry-on bags or on your person). The device must be in the off position and disconnected from the charging system and battery during all points of travel. Remove all electronic cigarette and vaping devices from carry-on bags if checked at the gate or planeside.

Vaporizer liquid is permitted in carry-on bags as long as it complies with the TSA liquid regulations, and is less than 3.4 oz and contained in a one quart plastic bag with other liquids as per the 3-1-1 guidelines.

Charging of the device and its batteries is prohibited on the aircraft. JetBlue does not permit smoking of the e-cigarettes at gate areas when embarking, disembarking or while inside the aircraft at any times.

Need help?

Emergency Exit Rows

Emergency doors are often heavy and awkward to lift, push, pull and maneuver. For the safety of all customers, federal regulations require customers seated in an emergency exit row be able to assist our inflight crewmembers in the event of an emergency.

Exit row requirements

An exit row seat is each seat that has direct access to an exit, and each seat in a row of seats through which customers would have to pass to gain access to an exit.

All customers sitting in the exit row must be willing and able to meet the following exit row requirements:

  • Must have sufficient mobility, strength or dexterity in both arms and hands, and both legs. 
  • Be 15 years of age or older and have the capacity to perform the listed duties without the assistance of an adult companion, parent, or other relative.  
  • Be able to read and understand instructions related to emergency evacuations which are provided in printed, hand-written or graphic form and be able to understand oral commands given by a crewmember in the English language. 
  • Be able to see well enough to perform the listed duties without the assistance of visual aids; may wear contacts or eyeglasses. 
  • Be able to hear well enough to understand instructions shouted by crewmembers without assistance; may wear hearing aids. 
  • Be able to speak English well enough to give information verbally to other customers. 
  • Be free from the use of a walking assistive device (cane, walker, wheelchair, and/or crutches). 
  • Be free from responsibilities that require care for another customer, such as a small child or someone physically or mentally unable to care for themselves, which might prevent them from performing any of the listed duties. 
  • Be free from the responsibilities required by the presence of a pet or service animal. 
  • Be free from any condition that might cause harm if they were to perform one or more of the listed duties. 

Airport crewmembers make the final determination of who will be seated in exit row seats, even when seats have been assigned prior to arrival at the airport. 

 

Exit row locations

Airbus A220

Row 12 is the emergency exit row.

Airbus A320 Classic

Rows 10 and 11 are emergency exit rows. 

Airbus A320 Restyled

Rows 10 and 11 are emergency exit rows.

Airbus A321 Classic

Rows 9, 10, 22, and 23 are emergency exit rows.

Airbus A321 Classic with Mint®

Rows 6 and 18 are emergency exit rows. 

Airbus A321neo

Rows 16, 17, 24 and 25 are emergency exit rows. 

Airbus A321neo with Mint®

Rows 13 and 14 are emergency exit rows.

Airbus A321 Long Range

Rows 13 and 14 are emergency exit rows.
