Flight delays, cancellations and schedule changes

Understand whether you’re affected, explore your options, and decide what to do next.

What best describes your situation?

  • Is my flight affected?

    Not every travel alert impacts every flight.

    • Start by checking your flight status Flight Tracker will show whether your flight is on time, delayed, or canceled.
    • If your flight is on time No action is needed. Continue monitoring your flight for updates as your travel date approaches.
    • If your flight is delayed or canceled Review your itinerary and available options in Manage Trips.

  • My flight is delayed

    • My updated departure time still works No action is needed. We’ll keep you on your existing flight.
    • I need to change my plans Review the options available for your reservation in Manage Trips.
    • Check for updates Check Flight Tracker for the latest status and gate updates.

  • My flight was cancelled

    • Check your updated itinerary first JetBlue may have automatically rebooked you on another available flight. Check Manage Trips to review your updated itinerary.
    • If you’re happy with your updated itinerary No further action is needed.
    • If you need a different option Review the available options in Manage Trips. If you decide not to travel, you may also be eligible for a refund.

  • Will I receive a refund?

    If your flight has been canceled or significantly changed by JetBlue, you may be eligible for a refund. Eligible refunds may include:

    • Your original airfare.
    • Paid seat selections.
    • Checked bag fees.
    • Pet fees and other eligible ancillary purchases.

    If you’d rather continue traveling, you may also have rebooking options available. Review what’s available for your reservation in Manage Trips. For complete refund eligibility view our Refund Policy.

Disruptions

If your flight is disrupted for any of the following reasons: 

  • Cancelled flights with no available alternative within 24 hours
  • The departure is moved earlier by 3+ hours (domestic) or 6+ hours (international)
  • The arrival is delayed by 3+ hours (domestic) or 6+ hours (international)
  • The flight is changed to a different departure or arrival airport
  • Additional connections are added to the itinerary
  • The customer is moved from Mint to Main
  • A schedule change results in a misconnect or does not meet minimum connect times

We will keep you on your existing flight (if delayed) or automatically rebook you onto a new flight (if canceled). If you’re satisfied with your flight, no further action is needed.

If you’re not satisfied, you are eligible to:

  • Rebook on an alternate flight within 5 days at no additional cost or
  • Cancel and refund to the original form of payment, including ancillary fees (seats, pets, bags, etc.). To be eligible for a refund, the customer must cancel online or contact us prior to flight departure.

Was your flight delayed or cancelled?

Manage your trip to change or cancel flights, add bags, & more. Be sure to check for email notifications from JetBlue about your trip to keep you informed. If you do not see an email, check your spam folder.

Customers may check their flight status using the online Flight Tracker. This tool is also available on the JetBlue mobile app.

Flights Cancelled by JetBlue

If your flight is cancelled by JetBlue for any reason and you had a confirmed reservation at the time of cancellation, the following options are available:

  • Rebook on the next available JetBlue flight at no additional charge
  • Receive a refund to the original form of payment.

Additionally, flights cancelled within 4 hours of scheduled departure are eligible for compensation under our Customer Bill of Rights.

Heads up: Beginning in early 2026, Customer Bill of Rights compensation will be issued as TrueBlue points instead of a JetBlue travel credit. To receive points, customers must have a TrueBlue account attached to their reservation prior to departure.

I already cancelled my flight before the fee waiver was issued

If you voluntarily changed or cancelled your flight before a fee waiver was issued and the flight was still scheduled to operate, the original fare rules will apply. All fees and fare differences will apply.

  • More info if you already cancelled your flight

    If you chose to change your flight due to a JetBlue delay, you voluntarily give up compensation for the flight disruption, as JetBlue put you on another available flight. 

    • For example, if your flight was delayed for 3 hours and you choose to take an earlier flight that day, you will no longer qualify for compensation for the original flight. 
    • To qualify for compensation, you must have had a confirmed reservation on the flight that was impacted at the time when the delay or cancellation was issued. 
    • Only customers who have checked in for the JetBlue-operated flight and are listed on the flight at the time of departure or cancellation are eligible for compensation.
      • If you were originally listed on the flight and waited during the delay, but voluntarily change your flight before your originally scheduled flight departs or is cancelled, you are no longer eligible for compensation.

  • Rebooking a cancelled flight

    If your flight is cancelled by JetBlue for any reason, you may rebook your travel without a fee or increase in airfare under the Manage Trips section of jetblue.com or by contacting JetBlue. Please be prepared to provide the following:

    • Confirmation number 
    • Flight number
    • Date(s) of travel
    • Customer information (such as full name, date of birth, contact information, etc.)

    If we are able to move you to a different flight after your original flight is cancelled, you may already see a new itinerary when you view your reservation through the JetBlue mobile app or Manage Trips. 

Weather Disruptions

We will do our best to stick to our schedules, rain or shine. Weather can sometimes cause delays with your flight or with airports and in those situations, we have plans and options to help with your

Any weather-related alerts or fee waivers will be posted at the top of our website.

  • What is a fee waiver?

    A fee waiver is a change/cancel fee exception issued during times of major disruption (like impactful weather).

  • What do I do with a fee waiver?

    In the event that a fee waiver has been issued, impacted customers may change or cancel their flight without a fee or increase in airfare using Manage Trips. If you need additional assistance, contact us.

    • You won’t be charged if you change or cancel your flight.
    • Only applies to flights during a specified eligibility period.
      • Check the travel alerts page for information about any existing fee waivers.
    • For some international destinations, certain taxes are nonrefundable. 
    • Fare difference may apply in certain situations. 

  • What if there is no fee waiver?

    JetBlue does not waive fees based on impending storms unless a fee waiver has been issued, a flight delay of 3 hours or more for a domestic and 6 hours or more for an international or a flight cancellation has occurred. 

Disrupted by weather?

If a fee waiver has been issued, log into Manage Trips to update your reservation. Manage Trips automatically incorporates the details of a fee waiver if your trip meets the criteria.

More details about weather disruptions.
  • Customer safety is always our first priority
  • For weather-related cancellations, we make every effort to cancel flights more than four hours before departure but depending on the circumstances, this is not always possible
  • Weather anywhere along a flight’s route can cause a delay or cancellation
  • Different types of weather conditions may not be visible to the naked eye, such as wind and in-cloud thunderstorms
  • Weather impacts all airlines differently depending on airport locations, hubs, and flight paths
  • Weather in one city may cause flight disruptions for one airline but not another
  • If a flight is cancelled it will not be reinstated
  • Download the JetBlue mobile app to use the Where’s my plane? feature to track your incoming plane.
  • Use the flight tracker to track any JetBlue flight.

Air Traffic Control Delays

  • What is an ATC delay and why is my flight delayed?

    When there is bad weather in the forecast (which could be over a destination that you are traveling to or along the route) the FAA will evaluate whether it needs to issue an Air Traffic Control (ATC) program. 

    ATC programs come in different shapes and sizes. Some programs help reroute aircraft around the weather, other programs may increase the spacing between aircraft and some may prohibit aircraft from operating. When this happens, it slows everything down (or stops flights) and this is when you see flight delays and cancellations. 

    Sometimes the FAA issues ATC programs when they do not have enough Air Traffic Controllers to handle the volume of flights. These controllers work hard but sometimes they need to slow things down to safely manage the volume of aircraft in particular region or nationwide. 

  • Can I get compensated for an ATC delay?

    Most ATC delays are not eligible for compensation.

  • How do I know if there is an ATC delay at my airport or en route?

    The Federal Aviation Administration (FAA) issues Air Traffic Control (ATC) programs. 

  • Why is there an ATC delay when my airport is sunny?

    An airline is a network of complex moving pieces and when one part of the network gets disrupted, it can have a cascading impact if the disruption goes on for too long. 

    For example, if there is a 3-hour ATC delay from JFK to Syracuse because of weather at JFK, that plane in JFK will be delayed. If the flight has the same plane and same crew, the return flight from Syracuse back to JFK will also be delayed. Customers at JFK who look out the window and see bad weather are probably fairly understanding. On the flip side, for customers in Syracuse where it may be sunny, it is hard to understand why the flight is delayed. 

    Once that plane is released to fly to Syracuse, we must ensure that the crew have enough time left to operate both legs of the flight. 

    If we do not believe they will have enough time to operate, we will replace the crew. Replacing a crew is far easier in larger cities where we have the reserve resources. If we determine that a crew has a good amount of time left to operate the flight, we will continue with them on that flight. 

    However, even if the crew have sufficient times, other unexpected things can happen such as more ATC route closures or a pop-up thunderstorm in Syracuse. These things could then create a cascade of challenges including the crew timing out and the flight cancelling. None of this is optimal and we do our best to use all available information at the time to set each flight up for success. 

  • Why does my crew time out when there is an ATC delay?

    Most airlines can handle a certain number of ATC delays because we carry extra resources such as pilots and flight attendants. 

    Importantly, pilots and flight attendants are limited by law in the number of hours they can work (even if they are delayed on the ground and not inflight) so at some point they are simply not permitted to continue working. At JetBlue, we are constantly projecting out when the pilots and flight attendants might reach their hours limit so we can replace them.   If ATC programs are prolonged because, for example, the weather proves to be worse than forecast (pop-up thunderstorms in the summer are very challenging to forecast both timing and location) or the FAA is insufficiently staffed to handle the volume or complexity of a particular day, an airline’s extra resources begin to run out and you start to see cancellations and cascading delays. 

    This summer, JetBlue is carrying nearly 20% more flight attendants and 50% more pilots in reserve than last summer (and 30% and 80% more, respectively compared to June 2019) and even with these significantly higher resources, lengthy ATC delays are proving a challenge to overcome.  

    We work closely with the FAA to plan each day. However, sometimes the unexpected can happen.

  • Is there an Air Traffic Control Delay?

    Log into Manage Trips to update your reservation. 

Flights Changed by JetBlue (Schedule Changes)

If your flight is changed by JetBlue, the following options are available based on the extent of the change.

Change less than 3 hours for domestic or less than 6 hours for international.

Any additional voluntary changes you wish to make will be subject to the increase in airfare and any applicable fee. OR Flights qualifying for any current fee waiver may be changed or cancelled without a fee.

Change more than 3 hours for domestic or more than 6 hours for international.

You may travel on another JetBlue flight within 5 days before or after your original flight at no additional charge OR You may cancel and receive a refund to the original form of payment

Change from nonstop to connecting/stopover flight.

You may travel on another JetBlue flight within 5 days before or after your original flight at no additional charge OR You may cancel and receive a refund to the original form of payment

Other Reasons for Delays

Some other reasons for delays can include:

  • Safety delays
    • At JetBlue, safety is our #1 priority. If we identify an opportunity to improve any safety aspect of your trip, we may delay or cancel a flight. 
  • Airport logistics
  • Unexpected operational impacts

In the event of a delay or cancelled flight, log into Manage Trips to update your reservation. 

Not impacted by a delay?

If you want to change your flight outside of a delayed or cancelled flight, visit our voluntary changes page.

See how to change your flight

Other Important Info

  • If your flight is delayed or cancelled, JetBlue will contact you by email and an automated phone call.
  • For delays, we suggest you arrive at the airport for the originally scheduled departure time. If conditions allow, the estimated departure time could be moved up. The flight is not able to wait for customers who are not there.
  • For cancellations, you can change your outbound or return flight to ensure your trip length stays the same. Contact JetBlue for assistance.
  • If eligible, JetBlue may be able to rebook customers whose flights are cancelled or delayed 3 hours or more for domestic and 6 hours or more for international onto a partner airline. 
  • If a flight is cancelled, it will not be reinstated.

Still need help? Contact us.

Frequently Asked Questions

  • JetBlue changed my flight. How do I find my new itinerary? Will I be compensated?

    • If your reservation does not pull up in the app, try in your desktop browser.  
    • If that does not work, contact a crewmember to assist you with your reservation.   
    • Even if you accept the schedule change, you are still able to make one change to your itinerary with no fee, in some cases. See details above in the “Flights changed by JetBlue (schedule changes)” section. 
    • Per our Contract of Carriage, when operational schedule/flight changes occur, we can assist you within our policy, but JetBlue does not compensate for schedule changes.

  • JetBlue delayed my flight. Can I be accommodated on another airline’s flight?

    JetBlue may be able rebook customers whose flights are cancelled or are delayed 3 hours or more for domestic and 6 hours or more for international onto a partner airline. 

    Contact us. 

  • What should I do if my flight is delayed or cancelled?

    • Contact us to change your connecting flight.
    • Review our policy on delays.
    • Learn more about cancellations.

  • JetBlue changed or cancelled my flight. What happens to my TrueBlue Travel add-ons?

    Your TrueBlue Travel car, stay, or activity purchased through TrueBlue Travel will not automatically be changed or cancelled when your flights are changed or cancelled, whether the flights were booked through JetBlue or a third party.

    If you need to cancel or modify your existing TrueBlue Travel purchase, visit TrueBlue Travel or call 1-855-860-2583 for assistance. Changes and cancellations may be subject to travel supplier fees.

    If you wish to keep your purchase despite a cancelled flight, you can! Our TrueBlue Travel helpful humans also review changes made to flights more than 48 hours before departure and may proactively call you to verify if you still need your car, stay, or activity.

  • Extreme weather is hitting my destination. Am I able to change travel dates without a fee?

    • JetBlue monitors upcoming weather events and plans accordingly. View our page for more information on current fee waivers being offered due to inclement weather or other events.  
    • Get more information on current fee waivers.  
    • Get more information on weather disruptions.

  • What is the status of my flight?

    • For information on possible delays and cancellations for your specific flight, watch your inbox for email notifications.   
    • You can also use our Flight Tracker tool to look up the status of your flight at any time. 

  • My flight was cancelled due to extreme weather. Will I be compensated?

    • Based on the specific circumstances of the weather, fee waivers will be issued as needed. See the Travel Alerts page for applicable fee waivers.   
    • Customers with cancelled flights may opt for a refund to the original form of payment, if travel was booked on or before the dates specified under the fee waiver.

  • I am stranded abroad due to extreme weather. What do I do?

    • Use our easy online Manage Trips tool or contact us by chat or phone for rebooking assistance.
    • Please review your options for travel insurance.  
    • Check to see if your credit card provides travel insurance.  
    • If you purchased insurance through Allianz, please contact them online or call 1-800-496-0329.

Gate Passes

Requesting a Gate Pass

Gate passes may be requested for a non-ticketed companion for:

  • Unaccompanied minors
  • Translation assistance for speakers of other languages
  • Customers requiring assistance related to a disability
    • We provide escort from point to point only; we do not provide constant supervision
    • Requests must be made at the airport during check-in by providing the name of the person for pick-up

Gate passes are issued based on TSA’s discretion and security recommendation. They are not guaranteed. You can check availability at the JetBlue airport ticket counter. The person requesting the gate pass will need to have their government-issued photo identification with them.   Gate passes are not available in the following situations:

  • To meet someone at the gate when their flight is arriving from an international location.
  • To accompany someone to the gate for flights departing from a city outside the United States.
Need help?

Get your boarding pass

Can’t find your boarding pass? Need to make corrections? We can help. Check out our most frequently asked questions below.

Finding, printing, sending boarding passes

  • How do I print/view my boarding pass? Where is it located?

    • The JetBlue app - review your upcoming trip.  With this option, you can also save your boarding pass to your Apple Wallet for easy access.  Download the free JetBlue app to access your self-scannable boarding pass.
    • Fill out your information on the Manage Trips page, hit enter to go to the ‘My Booking’ page, then use the Get Boarding Pass option on the bottom of your My Booking page to view and print your boarding pass.    
    • At the airport, print your boarding pass at the kiosk or ticket counter. 
    • Check in with JetBlue or a partner airline - whichever is the first flight on your itinerary.   
    • For third party bookings,use the same check-in options to access your boarding pass.
    • Connecting flight boarding passes are issued via app, email or at the airport when you check in if you have seat assignments on both segments of your flights.  

     

    • If you are placed on the standby list for the first flight, you will only receive the first segment boarding pass. 
    • When you are issued a seat assignment for that segment, that will prompt the connecting boarding pass to print too.    

     

    • For group bookings,you are required to check in at the ticket counter with an Airport Crewmember. All boarding passes will be issued at that time.  

  • How do I email my itinerary to myself or others?

    • On the Manage Your Trip page, look above your flight details.
    • Share your booking details by using the “Save as PDF” icon. (The icon image looks like a piece of paper with a folded edge). Select the icon.
    • A copy of your reservation will be downloaded as a PDF file. Click on it. 
    • Share by emailing or texting the pdf file to yourself or others. 
    • NOTE: This downloaded info. will not include your boarding pass. Go to the  JetBlue app to see your boarding pass or print it at a JetBlue airport kiosk. 

  • How do I get a boarding pass for my connecting flight?

    • Connecting boarding passes are issued when you check in, if you have seat assignments on both segments of your flights.  
    • If you are placed on the standby list for the first flight, you will only receive the first segment boarding pass. 
    • When you are issued a seat assignment for that segment, that will prompt the connecting boarding pass to print too. It will be available at the gate or on your app.

  • Not checking bags?

    If you already have your boarding pass (mobile or paper), you don’t need to stop at the kiosk but you can if you want to change seats, purchase EvenMore®, extra legroom seats, Even More Speed, add your TrueBlue or Known Traveler numbers, or even add a pet (subject to space availability on flight), with just a few clicks. 

    Get your boarding pass.

Make changes

  • How do I correct information on my boarding pass?

    • Information on the boarding pass reflects what is on your reservation.
    • To change details on your boarding pass, go to Manage Trips then fill in your information, hit enter, access and update your Traveler Details.
    • If the correction cannot be fixed through Manage Trips, contact us for assistance.

  • How do I add TSA PreCheck to my boarding pass?

    • Add your TSA PreCheck Known Traveler Number by entering your information on the Manage Trips page, then using the Update Traveler Details option on the bottom of the page. 
    • We suggest you add your TSA PreCheck at least 24 hours prior to your flight.  
    • Verify the following: 
      • Known traveler number was entered correctly. 
      • Name on your reservation, date of birth and gender exactly match the info. attached to your traveler number. 
      • TSA PreCheck enrollment has not expired.  
    • As a reminder, TSA PreCheck is not guaranteed for every flight.  
    • Visit our TSA Pre✓® page, the Transportation Security Administration’s TSA PreCheck page, or contact TSA at 866-289-9673. 

  • Already checked in - but need to make a change? Uncheck in.

    • In order to make a change after checking in, you’ll need to “uncheck in.” Yes – that is a thing. Unchecking unlocks your reservation so you can change your flight plans yourself.

    • To uncheck:

      • Go to the jetblue.com homepage and select Check in 

      • Add your name and confirmation code or ticket number 

      • Select Find Flights and Continue 

      • Select Travelers to be Unchecked and then Cancel Check-in

      • Follow prompt to complete the process

      • You should see a notification that says you are unchecked. Proceed to the Manage Trips tool to rebook or make changes yourself.

         

        Note: If you are unable to uncheck yourself, please reach out for assistance. If you are already at the airport, go to the airport ticket counter for assistance.

        You will not be able to uncheck yourself within 30 minutes of your scheduled departure time. 

  • Does my infant get a boarding pass?

    Yes. When you check in, lap infants receive a boarding pass with the indicator INFT under their seat assignment. Their boarding pass will not have a ticket number. 

     

Troubleshooting issues

  • I filled out my attestation form, but got an error message when checking in.

    If you did not get errors while completing your attestation form, try to check in and print your boarding pass at the airport kiosk or with an airport crewmember.  

  • The system is telling me to “See agent at airport.” What does that mean?

    We recommend that you arrive as soon as possible to have your reservation reviewed. There may be a delay, wrong information, or it may be too late to check in for your flight online.  

  • I tried to get my boarding pass, but it was too late. I missed my flight.

    • We suggest that you speak with an Airport Crewmember regarding rebooking your flight.  
      • Missed flights are not eligible for denied boarding compensation.  
    • Go to the JetBlue Just Ask desk in larger cities for rebooking if you are inside security.

Baggage solutions

  • How can I print a bag tag? Can I print it at home?

    • Print bag tags for your luggage at curbside check-in, an airport kiosk, or at the ticket counter with an airport crewmember.  
    • Bag tags cannot be printed at home.

  • My checked bags are not showing on my boarding pass.

    • Proof of checked baggage will not appear on your boarding pass.  
    • When your bags are tagged, you will receive bag tag receipts – one for each checked bag.

Group Bookings

Because traveling with others is more fun!

Families and groups less than 8

If you are booking as a family or small group and are ready to purchase your tickets, you can book online by creating multiple reservations of up to seven people at a time.

Book your small group here

Groups of 8-9

If you are booking a group with 8-9 travelers, please contact us for assistance.

Groups of 10 or more

If you are booking a sports, church, youth or similar group of at least 10 travelers, JetBlue’s group bookings are designed with you in mind. To book a group, contact the JetBlue Groups Desk.

JetBlue Groups Desk Monday thru Friday 8am to 7:30pm ET (5am to 4:30pm PT) 1-888-JETBLUE (1-888-538-2583), option 2

Please note the following when considering a group booking:

  • JetBlue groups start at a minimum of 10 people traveling on the same flights.
  • Fare quotes are valid for up to two weeks when booking 120 days or more from departure, depending on dates of travel and time of quote.
  • Payment for group bookings, including deposits and fees, is only collected in U.S. dollars.
  • Seats are held with a nonrefundable deposit of $50 per seat until 30 days prior to departure (60 days on international bookings within North and South America). For transatlantic bookings the nonrefundable deposit is $150 per seat. 
  • Traveler information is due 30 days prior to travel.
  • Cancellation of seats once a deposit payment has been made will result in forfeiture of all monies paid.

JetBlue does not discount group fares or honor lower fares on group bookings.

JetBlue group guidelines

The following guidelines for group bookings take precedence over JetBlue’s general fare rules and restrictions.

Please note: Individuals in group bookings are not able to check in online, through the JetBlue mobile app, or at an airport kiosk. When you arrive at the airport, please proceed to the ticket counter and a crewmember will be happy to assist you with your check in.

You can submit a request for a group booking on our website. Once you do, one of our dedicated groups crewmembers will contact you within the next few business days with a quote and help answer any questions you may have.

Please keep in mind that submitting the online request does not hold space or guarantee availability or fares.

Group size qualifications
  • 10 or more customers (i.e. sports, youth, church, school, or community groups).
  • All group customers must be booked on the same flight on the same day. Customers traveling separate from the group are considered individual bookings and regular fare rules and restrictions apply, including unaccompanied minor fees.
  • All groups with minors under the age of 18 must have adequate chaperones traveling with the group on the same reservation. Chaperones must be 18 years of age or older.
Deposit
  • For flights in North and South America, a nonrefundable deposit of $50 per person, per reservation is required within 14 days of the date of booking when booking 120 days or more prior to departure.
  • For transatlantic flights, a nonrefundable deposit of $150 per person is due within 14 days of the booking date when booking 120 days or more prior to departure.
  • Your deposit is applied toward your final payment.
  • JetBlue may cancel the reservation if your deposit is not received by the due date.
Payment info
  • Final payment is due 30 days prior to departure for domestic travel.
  • Final payment is due 60 days prior to departure for international and transatlantic travel.
  • If final payment is not received by the due date, JetBlue may cancel the reservation(s) without notice and all monies paid are forfeited.
Payment types
  • Payment for group bookings, including deposits and fees, is only collected in U.S. dollars. Foreign currency conversion rates may vary.
  • We accept most major credit cards, personal checks, agency checks and money orders. Your contract will provide specific details.
  • We’re limited to four payment transactions per group.
  • Payments must be in our Salt Lake City, UT office on or before the payment due date.
  • Other payment restrictions may apply depending on your dates of travel.
Name guidelines

Customer names

  • All customer names are due 30 days prior to departure for domestic, international, and transatlantic travel.
  • If names are not received by the due date, JetBlue may cancel the reservation without notice. Cancellation fees apply.

Name changes

  • Name changes can be made for a fee of $100 per name, plus any applicable taxes and fees. Name changes must be completed at least 72 hours prior to departure.
  • Name corrections can be made at no additional charge and must be completed at least 72 hours prior to departure.
Deviations
  • After final payment has been made and the itinerary has been ticketed, individual deviations from the group are allowed for a fee of $135 per person plus the increase in airfare. Your group will be allowed to deviate up to 20% of the total group size.
  • New travel dates are permitted two weeks prior to or after departure.
  • Any deviation is subject to the JetBlue change fee plus any increase in airfare.
  • All deviations must be requested by the group leader.
Changes, cancellations, fees and refunds
  • For domestic travel, changes/cancellations made within 30 days of departure will result in complete forfeiture of the monies paid.
  • For international and transatlantic travel, changes/cancellations made within 60 days of departure will result in complete forfeiture of the monies paid.
  • Any domestic, international, or transatlantic group booking that is changed or cancelled after deposit is paid but prior to making full payment is charged a $135 change fee per person plus any increase in airfare.
  • If a change is made after full payment is made but outside of 30 or 60 days (depending on the destination) from departure, it is subject to a $135 change fee per person plus any increase in airfare. If a change after full payment results in a lower fare, the difference will be forfeited.
  • All payments are non-refundable.
  • If your group drops below 10 customers prior to making full payment, the reservation(s) is cancelled and rebooked at current fare, forfeiting any deposits that have already been paid. Fees will apply. The general fare rules and restrictions will apply to the new reservation.
  • All changes must be made by calling the JetBlue Groups Desk. 
Additional tickets/Internet bookings
  • Tickets added after original group reservation is made will be at the current fare, and payment in full may be required at time of booking.
  • Tickets booked on the internet will not be considered part of the group, and cannot be added to the group. Internet bookings are subject to our general fare rules and restrictions, including unaccompanied minor fees.
  • We are unable to add minors who have been booked on separate reservations to the group booking.
Seat assignments
  • A block of seats will be assigned after deposit has been made. We will do our best to seat your group together based on seat availability at time of deposit. Seat assignments are not guaranteed and are subject to change.
  • After final payment and customer names have been received and the seats ticketed, you may purchase EvenMore® or extra legroom seating. Payment for purchase of EvenMore® seating is due at the time of purchase.
TrueBlue and Mosaic
  • TrueBlue numbers cannot be added to group reservations.
  • Mosaic benefits cannot be applied to group reservations.
  • Frequent flyer numbers from other airline programs cannot be added to group reservations.
  • You can request points for any JetBlue-operated flight that you have taken within 12 months of the flight date. Sign in to your TrueBlue account after travel and use the Request missing points link.

Help with Booking a Flight

Having trouble booking?

Need your trip details?
  • Visit Manage Trips. 
Need to book a flight?
  • Explore flights.
  • Check out the Best Fare Finder. 
  • Book a flight with points. 
  • To see where JetBlue flies, visit the route map. 
  • Need trip inspo? Check out flight deals. 

Common Booking Issues

Payment issues

If your credit card is not working, try the following: 

  • Disable any pop-up blocker(s). 
  • Check to make sure you have enough left in your line of credit. 
  • You might have a limit to how much you can spend on one transaction or during a specific period of time.  
  • Check the security settings on the payment portion of your credit card.  
Helpful payment tips: 
  • If you have 15 minutes or more of inactivity while trying to book a flight, your session might time out. Please start from the beginning and complete the transaction without pausing.  
  • You may use a maximum of 2 forms of payment in a single transaction.  
    • Credit card + points 
    • Credit card + travel credit 
    • If you need to pay with two or more credit cards or multiple forms of payment for your reservation, please contact us.
    • See all forms of payment. 
JetBlue’s Network
  • JetBlue flies to over 100 destinations in the U.S., Latin America, Caribbean and Europe. 
  • If you don’t see your desired trip, that route might be seasonal or the connection times might not match up. Visit the Best Fare Finder to see available dates.  
  • See our flight schedule.  
Family seats

Want to sit next to your children when you fly? We’ll automatically place children 13 years old and younger with an accompanying adult, if seats are available, prior to check-in.    See family seating details for more information and the conditions that must be met. 

FAQs

  • Need to book a roundtrip or return flight?

    1. Start your search.
    2. Be sure the top left corner of the search form under “Feeling fly today?” says Roundtrip. 
    3. To change this, click the top left corner and select your trip type. You can choose from Roundtrip, One-way or Multi-city.
    4. When searching for a flight, roundtrip is often the default.

  • How do I book my seats?

    For Main Base fares:

    If you have a Main Base fare, prior to 24 hours before departure, you can reserve seats for a fee. If you choose not to reserve seats, you will be auto-assigned seats at no charge within 24 hours of departure if available. You can change auto-assigned seats for a fee.

    For Main, Main Flex, EvenMore, EvenMore Flex, Mint, or Mint Flex fares:

    You can select your seats during booking or sign into Manage Trips at any time.  

    See the Mint experience.

    Traveling on multiple airlines:

    If you booked your trip through JetBlue but are traveling on another airline for part of your trip, you will not be able to book those other airline seats on jetblue.com. Please see the other airline’s website to manage that leg of your trip.  

    Family seating:

    Want to sit by your kids when you fly? We’ve now automated the family seating process, and it auto-seats any children ages 13 and under with an accompanying adult as early as booking time, if seats are available. 

    See family seating details 

    EvenMore®, Preferred and extra legroom seats:

    Anyone can purchase EvenMore®, extra legroom, and Preferred seats during booking or in Manage Trips.  

  • Why can’t I book my own flights on my Visa credit card?

    If your credit card is not working, try the following:

    • Disable any pop-up blocker(s). 
    • Check to make sure you have enough left in your line of credit. 
      • You might have a limit to how much you can spend on one transaction or during a specific time period.  
    • Check the security settings on the payment portion of your credit card. 

  • How do I book a wheelchair?

    To help us accommodate your unique travel needs and provide an exceptional JetBlue experience, we recommend you book your travel as soon as possible and add a Special Service Request  (SSR). You can add an SSR when you book online at jetblue.com or with our free app. 

    Need help booking or adding a Special Service Request? Please contact us  or call us at 711 (TTY). There’s never a fee to add an SSR to an existing booking.  

    If you are at the airport, please let our airport crewmembers know how they can assist you.

  • How far in advance can I see flights and book them?

    You can now purchase flights as far out as 331 days from the date of booking at any time. Our flight schedules will automatically extend day-by-day. Check out our Best Fare Finder to see if we have flights scheduled during your desired dates for a chosen route. 

    See flight availability

Holy Water / Zamzam Water

We are proud to support your religions and traditions while you travel with us.

Holy Water & Zamzam Water on JetBlue Flights

JetBlue will accept one jerry can containing up to 10 liters (2.64 gallons) of Holy water or Zamzam water as a checked bag at no extra charge.

  • The jerry can must be properly packed in a plastic covering to avoid leakage and damage to other bags.
  • Jerry cans containing Holy or Zamzam water are not permitted as a carry-on bag.
  • If more than one jerry can is checked, the extra jerry cans will be subject to standard excess bag charges.
Need help?

How to Redeem Travel Certificates

Travel certificates provide confirmed travel with JetBlue.

Terms and conditions for travel certificates

Certificate travel is always subject to availability and blackout dates. Change/cancel fees apply per person. Travel must be booked and completed prior to the expiration date listed on the travel certificate. 

Travel certificates are not valid for bookings on partner or codeshare/interline flights.

Each travel certificate includes specific terms and conditions, such as whether it can be used for one-way or roundtrip travel, domestic or international destinations, and when it expires. These details are found in the travel authorization letter that was emailed to you, which also includes instructions on booking your reservation. 

A major credit card will be required for any applicable taxes and fees. JetBlue travel credits cannot be used with a travel certificate.

Information needed for new booking requests

Travel certificate requests must be completed a minimum of seven (7) days in advance of the desired date of travel. Remember to add any TrueBlue numbers, special requests, passport information and Known Traveler numbers during booking.

If your travel certificate has a six-digit authorization (numbers only): 

  • Please follow the instructions on your travel certificate.
  • When requesting assistance with a new travel certificate booking, please have the following details available:
    • Traveler’s full legal name
    • Traveler’s date of birth
    • Traveler’s contact details (address, phone number, email address)
    • 3 different flight options including cities, dates, and flight numbers
    • Travel certificate authorization and PIN

If your travel certificate has a 16-digit authorization (letters and numbers):

Please follow the instructions on your travel certificate. It will include a unique link that can be used to book and manage your reservation. 

Blackout dates

Blackout dates are subject to change and certificate travel is always subject to availability. In addition to the dates listed below, availability can be limited on weekends.
